# AndroidBooks — catalog and extraction coverage

> Generated from every `.pdf` and `.epub` found recursively under `E:\AndroidBooks` on 2026-11-21. This is a source map and coverage record, not a replacement copy of any book.

- Files scanned: **150** (76 PDF, 74 EPUB).
- Unique SHA-256 contents: **139**; exact duplicate groups: **11**.
- Extracted text: **14,269,536 words** counting duplicate files; **13,661,831** when exact binary copies are counted once. Extraction volume is not proof of human page-by-page review.
- Page-level extraction errors: **1**; inspect the notes at the end.
- PDF extraction preserves searchable text but not page layout, diagrams, code screenshots, or every font encoding. Rows marked `PARTIAL` need visual/OCR follow-up or a matching EPUB.
- Chapter headings below are PDF bookmarks where available and EPUB spine headings; they are navigation aids, not guaranteed complete printed tables of contents.

## How to use this catalog

Use the `[B###]` identifier and relative path to identify a source. Exact duplicates share a hash group. Prefer the copy with readable text; do not reprocess exact byte-identical duplicates. For version-sensitive implementation claims, cross-check the official documentation and current AndroidX/Kotlin source linked by the companion notes.
